"""Authenticated ECW public-port routing to guest loopback."""

import space_smoke as smoke

PORT = 8123
EXPECTED_BODY = "space-smoke-loopback-only-ok"


@smoke.scenario(
    "ecw.public_port_to_loopback",
    sandbox=smoke.sandbox_with_authenticated_public_ports(),
)
def public_port_to_loopback(test: smoke.Ecw) -> None:
    """An authenticated ECW public-port request reaches an HTTP service
    listening only on guest loopback and returns its exact response.
    """

    service = test.serve_http(
        listening_on=smoke.LOOPBACK_ONLY,
        port=PORT,
        response=EXPECTED_BODY,
    )

    response = service.request_from_public_port("/index.html")

    test.expect_http(response, status=200, body=EXPECTED_BODY)

FILE: space_smoke/scenarios/ecw_refresh_keepalive.py
================================================
"""ECW refresh keepalive invariant."""

import space_smoke as smoke

TIMEOUT_SECONDS = 30
REFRESH_AFTER_SECONDS = 20
VERIFY_AFTER_REFRESH_SECONDS = 17


@smoke.scenario(
    "ecw.refresh_keepalive",
    sandbox=smoke.SandboxConfig(timeout=TIMEOUT_SECONDS),
)
def refresh_keepalive(test: smoke.Ecw) -> None:
    """Refreshing a sandbox resets its idle deadline and keeps the sandbox usable
    beyond its original deadline.
    """

    test.prove_idle_deadline_reset(
        reset_after=REFRESH_AFTER_SECONDS,
        verify_after_reset=VERIFY_AFTER_REFRESH_SECONDS,
        reset=test.sandbox.refresh,
    )

FILE: space_smoke/scenarios/ecw_timeout_rearm.py
================================================
"""ECW same-value timeout re-arm invariant."""

import space_smoke as smoke

TIMEOUT_SECONDS = 30
RESET_AFTER_SECONDS = 20
VERIFY_AFTER_RESET_SECONDS = 17


@smoke.scenario(
    "ecw.timeout_rearm",
    sandbox=smoke.SandboxConfig(timeout=TIMEOUT_SECONDS),
)
def timeout_rearm(test: smoke.Ecw) -> None:
    """Setting a sandbox timeout to its current value resets the idle deadline
    and keeps the sandbox usable beyond its original deadline.
    """

    test.prove_idle_deadline_reset(
        reset_after=RESET_AFTER_SECONDS,
        verify_after_reset=VERIFY_AFTER_RESET_SECONDS,
        reset=lambda: test.sandbox.set_timeout(TIMEOUT_SECONDS),
    )



================================================
FILE: space_smoke/scenarios/native_contract_command_validation.py
================================================
"""Command input rejection without executing malformed commands."""
from space_smoke.registry import scenario
from space_smoke.native_hardening import NativeHardeningFixture

@scenario("native_contract.command_validation", api_surfaces=("native",))
def command_validation(test: NativeHardeningFixture) -> None:
    """Malformed command arguments and nonpositive timeout fail admission."""
    sid = test.create_ready()
    outcomes = []
    for label, body in (
        ("empty_argv", {"command": []}),
        ("nul_argv", {"command": ["printf", "\u0000"]}),
        ("zero_timeout", {"command": ["true"], "timeout": "0s"}),
    ):
        response = test.request("POST", f"/v1/sandboxes/{sid}/commands", body=body,
                                idempotency_key=test.new_key(label))
        outcomes.append({"input": label, "status": response.status})
    test.context.verify(all(item["status"] in (400, 422) for item in outcomes),
                        "malformed commands were accepted", outcomes=outcomes)



================================================
FILE: space_smoke/scenarios/native_contract_idempotency_conflict.py
================================================
"""Native idempotency request fingerprint boundary."""
from space_smoke.registry import scenario
from space_smoke.native_hardening import NativeHardeningFixture

@scenario("native_contract.idempotency_conflict", api_surfaces=("native",))
def idempotency_conflict(test: NativeHardeningFixture) -> None:
    """An idempotency key cannot accept a different sandbox request."""
    key = test.new_key("create")
    first = test.create(idempotency_key=key)
    test.context.verify(first.status == 202, "create was not accepted", status=first.status)
    conflicting = test.create({"template": test.config.template, "metadata": {"purpose": "different"}}, idempotency_key=key)
    test.context.verify(conflicting.status == 409,
                        "changed request reused an idempotency key", status=conflicting.status)
    test.wait_operation(first.body["operation_id"])

"""V1 CIDR deny enforcement under default allow."""

import space_smoke as smoke

REACHABLE = "https://example.com/"
INSIDE = "https://1.0.0.1/"


@smoke.scenario(
    "network_policy.v1_cidr_deny",
    sandbox=smoke.network_policy_sandbox(
        internet=True,
        deny=("1.0.0.0/24",),
    ),
)
def v1_cidr_deny(test: smoke.Ecw) -> None:
    """A matching V1 CIDR deny overrides default allow."""

    test.network_policy.verify_egress(
        reachable=(REACHABLE,),
        blocked=(INSIDE,),
    )

================================================
FILE: space_smoke/scenarios/network_policy_v1_rules_survive_restore.py
================================================
"""V1 ECW network-policy enforcement across suspend and restore."""

import space_smoke as smoke

ALLOWED = "https://example.com/"
DENIED = "http://1.0.0.1/"
UNLISTED = "http://9.9.9.9/"


@smoke.scenario(
    "network_policy.v1_rules_survive_restore",
    sandbox=smoke.network_policy_sandbox(
        internet=False,
        allow=("example.com",),
        deny=("1.0.0.1/32",),
    ),
)
def v1_rules_survive_restore(test: smoke.Ecw) -> None:
    """A V1 allow/deny policy enforces default deny and retains the same
    behavior after the sandbox is suspended and restored.
    """

    with test.phase("verify V1 policy after creation"):
        test.network_policy.verify_egress(
            reachable=(ALLOWED,),
            blocked=(DENIED, UNLISTED),
        )

    with test.phase("suspend and restore sandbox"):
        test.pause()
        test.reconnect_by_id()

    with test.phase("verify V1 policy after restore"):
        test.network_policy.verify_egress(reachable=(ALLOWED,), blocked=(DENIED,))

FILE: space_smoke/scenarios/network_policy_v1_tcp_dns_transport_respects_ip_allow.py

"""V1 TCP DNS transport enforcement under an exact-IP allow."""

import space_smoke as smoke


@smoke.scenario(
    "network_policy.v1_tcp_dns_transport_respects_ip_allow",
    sandbox=smoke.network_policy_sandbox(
        internet=False,
        allow=("1.1.1.1", "8.8.8.8", "example.com"),
    ),
)
def v1_tcp_dns_transport_respects_ip_allow(test: smoke.Ecw) -> None:
    """TCP DNS enforces reserved resolver IPs and queried domains independently."""

    test.network_policy.verify_dns_transport(
        "tcp",
        accepted=(
            ("1.1.1.1", "example.com"),
            ("8.8.8.8", "example.com"),
        ),
        refused=(
            ("1.1.1.1", "not-allowed.example"),
            ("8.8.8.8", "not-allowed.example"),
        ),
        blocked=(("9.9.9.9", "example.com"),),
    )

# Smoke recipe for the seed-based OCI overlay path.
#
# Usage:
#   space-builder build --recipe /space/templates/firecracker/recipes/oci-python-smoke.toml --id oci-python-smoke

[metadata]
name = "oci-python-smoke"
description = "Python 3.12 image overlaid onto the SPACE seed"
from = "oci-image"
base = "seed"
image = "docker.io/library/python:3.12-slim-bookworm"
vcpu = 2
memory_mib = 2048
disk_size_mb = 4096

[[steps]]
name = "Validate the image and SPACE user"
type = "run"
command = """
set -eu
python3 -c 'import sys; assert sys.version_info[:2] == (3, 12)'
grep -q '^VERSION_CODENAME=bookworm$' /etc/os-release
test "$(id -u user)" = 2000
install -d /opt/space
printf 'python-oci-smoke\n' > /opt/space/oci-python-smoke
"""

# Seed template recipe.
#
# Unlike higher-level templates such as code-runtime, seed starts from scratch:
# it downloads a base Ubuntu minimal cloud squashfs, customizes it into a
# Firecracker-friendly rootfs, and registers the resulting template locally.
#
# Usage:
#   space-admin-cli template build seed

[metadata]
name = "seed"
description = "Minimal Ubuntu Firecracker seed"
from = "scratch"

# The shared lineage carries a BPF/tracing-capable guest kernel. Every node
# running a template from this lineage must stage this exact kernel.
[guest_kernel]
version = "vmlinux-6.1.155-bpf-modsig"
digest = "sha256:15ea4431a450eedbfcb4b4d3d85dd8e941b829a0606db6cd0e0167530421f933"

[source]
type = "ubuntu-minimal-cloud-squashfs"
release = "resolute"
version = "26.04"
date = "release-20260908"

# Inter 4.1

The unmodified variable WOFF2 fonts and copyright/license notice come from the
[official Inter 4.1 release](https://github.com/rsms/inter/releases/tag/v4.1),
also used by SPACE's documentation. They are distributed under the SIL Open
Font License 1.1 in `LICENSE-Inter.txt`.

The release archive is
[`Inter-4.1.zip`](https://github.com/rsms/inter/releases/download/v4.1/Inter-4.1.zip),
SHA-256 `9883fdd4a49d4fb66bd8177ba6625ef9a64aa45899767dde3d36aa425756b11e`.

| File | SHA-256 |
| --- | --- |
| InterVariable.woff2 | 693b77d4f32ee9b8bfc995589b5fad5e99adf2832738661f5402f9978429a8e3 |
| InterVariable-Italic.woff2 | e564f652916db6c139570fefb9524a77c4d48f30c92928de9db19b6b5c7a262a |
| LICENSE-Inter.txt | 262481e844521b326f5ecd053e59b98c8b2da78c8ee1bdbb6e8174305e54935a |

`inter.css` declares regular and italic faces at weights 100–900. Serve these
files locally with the UI; no font CDN request is needed.

import { parseDashboardUrl } from '@/lib/dashboard-url';
import SandboxDetailPage from './sandbox-detail';

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';

export default function Page() {
  const configuredUrl = process.env.SPACE_UI_DATADOG_DASHBOARD_URL;
  const dashboardUrl = parseDashboardUrl(configuredUrl);
  if (configuredUrl?.trim() && !dashboardUrl) {
    console.warn(
      'SPACE_UI_DATADOG_DASHBOARD_URL must be an HTTPS URL without credentials; hiding the dashboard link',
    );
  }
  return <SandboxDetailPage dashboardUrl={dashboardUrl} />;
}

================================================
FILE: ui/src/lib/dashboard-url.ts
================================================
export function parseDashboardUrl(value: string | undefined): string | null {
  const configuredUrl = value?.trim();
  if (!configuredUrl || !URL.canParse(configuredUrl)) return null;

  const url = new URL(configuredUrl);
  if (url.protocol !== 'https:' || url.username || url.password) return null;
  return url.href;
}

FILE: ui/src/lib/sandbox-actions.ts
================================================
export const RESTORE_SANDBOX_REQUEST = {
  blocking: true,
  ready_timeout_secs: 60,
} as const;

export function sandboxLifecycleActionBody(action: string): unknown | undefined {
  if (action !== 'restore') {
    return undefined;
  }
  return RESTORE_SANDBOX_REQUEST;
}



================================================
FILE: ui/src/lib/sandbox-id.ts
================================================
const SANDBOX_LABEL_LEN = 8;
const PUBLIC_SANDBOX_ID_PREFIX = 'sbx_';

export function sandboxUuidPart(sandboxId: string): string {
  return sandboxId.startsWith(PUBLIC_SANDBOX_ID_PREFIX)
    ? sandboxId.slice(PUBLIC_SANDBOX_ID_PREFIX.length)
    : sandboxId;
}

export function publicSandboxIdFromAny(sandboxId: string): string {
  return sandboxId.startsWith(PUBLIC_SANDBOX_ID_PREFIX)
    ? sandboxId
    : `${PUBLIC_SANDBOX_ID_PREFIX}${sandboxId}`;
}

export function sandboxLabelOrId(sandboxId: string): string {
  const compact = sandboxUuidPart(sandboxId).replaceAll('-', '').toLowerCase();
  if (!compact || !/^[0-9a-f]+$/.test(compact)) {
    return sandboxId;
  }
  if (compact.length <= SANDBOX_LABEL_LEN) {
    return compact;
  }
  return compact.slice(-SANDBOX_LABEL_LEN);
}

FILE: ui/src/lib/table-pagination.ts
================================================
import type { TablePaginationConfig } from 'antd/es/table/interface';

export function tablePagination(label: string, defaultPageSize = 25): TablePaginationConfig {
  return {
    defaultPageSize,
    showSizeChanger: true,
    pageSizeOptions: ['10', '25', '50', '100'],
    showTotal: (total, range) => `${range[0]}-${range[1]} of ${total} ${label}`,
  };
}



================================================
FILE: .agents/plugins/AGENTS.md
================================================
# SPACE agent plugins

Plugin source lives under `.agents/plugins/<plugin>/`. Use this cross-agent layout:

```text
<plugin>/
├── plugin.yaml
├── skills/<skill>/SKILL.md
├── .claude-plugin/plugin.json
└── .codex-plugin/plugin.json
```

When adding or changing plugin content:

1. Keep `plugin.yaml`, `.claude-plugin/plugin.json`, and
   `.codex-plugin/plugin.json` identity, version, and description aligned.
2. Bump the plugin version in all three files when shipped content changes.
3. Update the repository marketplace entries when adding or renaming a plugin.
4. Run the applicable skill and plugin validators.



================================================
FILE: .agents/plugins/marketplace.json
================================================
{
  "name": "pplx-space",
  "interface": {
    "displayName": "SPACE"
  },
  "plugins": [
    {
      "name": "pplx-space",
      "source": {
        "source": "local",
        "path": "./.agents/plugins/pplx-space"
      },
      "policy": {
        "installation": "AVAILABLE",
        "authentication": "ON_INSTALL"
      },
      "category": "DevOps"
    }
  ]
}

================================================
FILE: .agents/plugins/pplx-space/skills/space-data-store-development/README.md
================================================
# SPACE data store development

This README is human-facing documentation. Agent instructions are limited to
`SKILL.md`; this file is not part of the skill's agent context.

## Inspect Postgres in EKS

Engineers performing an approved, read-only inspection can use the existing
Postgres bastion with an explicit cluster context:

```bash
kubectl --context <cluster-context> exec -n space deploy/space-postgres-bastion -- psql -c "<SQL>"
```

Keep queries read-only unless a cluster administrator explicitly approves the
specific write. Do not create ad hoc client pods or use the bastion for
application traffic, migrations, automation, or bulk exports.

  display_name: "SPACE Data Store Development"
  short_description: "Data stores, SQL, migrations, and fixtures"

FILE: .agents/plugins/pplx-space/skills/space-go-shim/SKILL.md
================================================
---
name: space-go-shim
description: Develop and validate the Go containerd shim under `shim/` and its shared socket contract.
---

# Develop the SPACE Go shim

Keep the shim's `sbxMgrSocket` constant in `shim/space.go` synchronized with
Rust's `SANDBOX_MANAGER_SOCKET` in
`crates/libs/common/core/src/host/linux.rs`.

Run all Go validation from `shim/` before committing or opening a PR:

```bash
gofmt -l .
go vet ./...
go test ./...
```

Apply `$space-code-quality` when adding tests or durable comments.

FILE: .cargo/config.toml
================================================
[alias]
lint = "clippy --workspace --locked -- -D warnings"
# Builds into the workspace target directory from any subdirectory. A caller that
# must not wait on a concurrent workspace build sets CARGO_TARGET_DIR itself.
arch = "run -q --locked -p pplx-space-archcheck --"

[target.aarch64-unknown-linux-musl]
linker = "aarch64-linux-musl-gcc"
rustflags = [
  "-C", "link-arg=-fuse-ld=mold",
]

[target.x86_64-unknown-linux-musl]
linker = "x86_64-linux-musl-gcc"
rustflags = [
  "-C", "link-arg=-fuse-ld=mold",
]
